Content note: Part 1 of the Notes: general description of the Unalaska islands and their natural history, by the missionary priest. Divisions: Part 1 (Section 1: general remarks on the Unalaska district: geography, nature); contents at the end. Editorial matter: contents list near the end. Cautions: Mostly natural history; the people and religion are in part 2. Part 3 (...87) not taken. About 66,278 words.

скте.
